** e.g. [[Enchanted Sword Rune]], [[Divine Crash]], [[Basic Spirit]], etc.

Air Pressure
======================================================================
Page ID: 1554
Wiki revision: 2026-09-24T19:08:33Z

Categories:
- Perks

[Wiki Text]
Category:Perks

[Raw Wikitext]
[[Category:Perks]]
{{PerkTemplate
| Description = Dealing Air Damage builds up the Buff ''Air Pressure'', the maximum of which scales on perk amount.
| Tags = Defensive, Buff, Offensive, Rune Activated, Rune, Armor
|Details =
<ul><li>Gain {{StatusEffects|Air Pressure}} when dealing <b>Air</b> damage, up to a maximum of <code>10 * perkAmount</code> potency</li>
<ul><li>Potency gained is equal to<code>10% + 1% * perkAmount</code> of damage dealt by attacks that contain <b>air</b> damage type before any modifiers</li>
<li>Duration is equal to <code>15</code> seconds</li>
<li>{{StatusEffects|Air Pressure}} increases defense by 1% per 1 potency</li>
<ul><li>Uses the <code>damageTaken/(1+defense%)</code> formula</li></ul></ul>
<li>Using a Rune while you have {{StatusEffects|Air Pressure}} will consume the status and release an Air Burst</li>
<li>Air Burst Base Damage is equal to <code>(6 + (0.65 * statusPotency))</code></li>
<ul><li>1.0 <b>Air</b> Damage Type</li>
<li>1.0 <b>Air</b> Scaling</li>
<li>Counts as a <b>Rune</b> hit</li>
<li>Can proc other effects</li></ul></ul>
|Notes =
<ul><li>Potency and Duration cannot be increased past their maximum</li>
<ul><li>i.e. Effects like [[Contained]] or [[Bastion Bless]] do not work on this perk</li></ul></ul>
|Preview=Air Pressure
|Sources = Stratos
}}

======================================================================
Adaptive Plate
======================================================================
Page ID: 1556
Wiki revision: 2026-08-12T11:04:19Z

Categories:
- Perks

[Wiki Text]
Category:Perks

[Raw Wikitext]
[[Category:Perks]]
{{PerkTemplate
     | Description = Taking an identical Type of Damage twice will activate a temporary holy bubble that grants Damage Reduction. Gain additional Damage Reduction against the specific Damage Types that triggered it.
     | Tags = Defensive, Armor
     |Details = <nowiki/>
*+10% defense against all damage while the bubble is active per 1 of the perk
**Uses the <code>damageTaken/(1+defense%)</code> formula
*+40% additional defense against the damage type(s) that procced the bubble per 1 of the perk
** i.e. +50% per perk against matched types
**Uses the <code>damageTaken/(1+defense%)</code> formula
*Bubble Duration is equivalent to <code>3 + 2 * perkAmount</code> seconds
     |Notes =
*Damage reduction works on self damage and can be procced by self damage.
*Damage reduction works on DoTs, but cannot be procced by DoTs.
*Stores damage type taken indefinitely until death or rejoining, meaning if you got hit by two '''Physical''' attacks 30 minutes apart with no other damage taken in between, the perk would still proc on '''Physical'''.
*This perk has no cooldown and can be procced again immediately after the current bubble expires.


'''Explanation:'''

Upon taking 2 consecutive hits from an attack with the same damage type(s) this perk activates, granting global damage reduction with additional damage reduction against attacks that deal exclusively the same damage type(s) as the one that activated the perk. i.e. If an attack dealt 2 damage types at once and that attack hit you twice successively, proccing the bubble, then the increased damage reduction only works against attacks that deal <b>BOTH</b> of the proccing damage types.

'''True''' DMG is treated as a separate damage type, but attacks that have had their damage type(s) converted to true will not count as '''True''' DMG for this perk but will count as their original damage type(s) (despite appearing as True DMG).

Additional damage reduction does not apply to constituent damage type(s) of '''Physical''' or '''Magic''' if '''Physical''' or '''Magic''' procced the perk. i.e. If '''Physical''' procced the bubble, then the additional damage reduction only works against pure '''Physical''' damage.
|Preview=Adaptive Plate
     |Sources = Northern Innovator
   }}

[Raw Wikitext]
[[Category:Perks]]
{{PerkTemplate
|Tags = Defensive, Armor
|Description = Ignore Stuns from Attacks below a certain threshold after accounting for modifiers. You gain a Damage Reduction against said Attacks.
|Details =
*Increases defense by +33.34% against attacks that do a 'weak stun'
**Uses the <code>damageTaken/(1+defense%)</code> formula
**e.g. provides '''~'''25% actual damage reduction
*Provides a conditional 0.2 seconds of stun resistance per 1 of this perk
|Notes =
*If <code>stun - stunRes ≤ bulwarkStunRes</code>, take no stun and reduce damage taken by <b>~</b>25%
**i.e. an attack does 0.5 seconds of stun and you have 0.3 Tenacity and 1 Bulwark, the actual stun is <code>0.5 - 0.3 = 0.2</code> seconds, and since 1 Bulwark is 0.2 seconds of stun resistance, and that’s equal to the actual stun received, it activates and makes you take no stun and reduces damage received
|Preview =
|Sources = Steel Sentry
}}

<ul><li>i.e. an attack with 0 proc coefficient has the same chance to crit as an attack with 1 proc coefficient, and an attack with 2 proc coefficient has double the critical chance of an attack with 1 proc coefficient</li></ul>

[Wiki Text]
Category:Perks 

__NOTOC__

[Raw Wikitext]
[[Category:Perks]] 
{{PerkTemplate
|Tags = Bleed Potency, Debuff, Armor, Weapon, Ring
|Description = Increases the damage of the bleed status by x%
|Details = 
<ul><li>Multiplies damage dealt by {{StatusEffects|Bleed}} by :</li>
<ul><li>When perkAmount is less than 10 : <code>((1 + perkAmount/10) ^ (1 + perkAmount/10))</code></li>
<li>When perkAmount is greater or equal to 10 : <code>((1 + perkAmount/10) ^ (2))</code></li></ul>
<li>Increases applied {{StatusEffects|Bleed}} potency by 0.1 per 1 of this perk</li></ul>
|Notes = 
<ul><li>Not directly increased by [[Cursed (Perk)|Cursed]] or [[Perk Effectiveness]], however they do increase the amount of the source which indirectly increases the amount of this perk</li>
<li>The value given by the in-game perk description is <span style="color:red; font-weight:bold;">incorrect</span> and does not reflect the actual damage boost granted by this perk, and uses the following formula :</li>
<ul><li>When perkAmount is less than 10 : <code>((perkAmount/10) ^ (1 + perkAmount/10)) * (1 + perkAmount/15)</code></li>
<li>When perkAmount is greater or equal to 10 : <code>((perkAmount/10) ^ (2)) * (1 + perkAmount/15)</code></li></ul></ul>
|Preview = 
|Sources = Icicle, Frozen Guard, Icicle Essence, Bloodied Needle, Bloodied Guard, Bloodied Essence, Dark Steel Mace, Spike Pole, Dark Steel Essence, Jagged Blade, Jagged Handle, Jagged Essence, Lord Vaen Blade, Lord Vaen Scythe Pole, Lord Vaen Scythe Essence, Serrated Blade, Serrated Handle, Serrated Essence, Bloodiron Sawblade, Bloodengine Handle, Sawblade Essence, Thorn Maiden, Winter Woof (Armor), Winter Woof Gloves, Profane, Plains Woof (Armor), Sharp Ring, Woof Fang Fist, Gremlin Gloves, Gremlin (Armor), Roguent Gloves, Caci Blade, Caci Handle, Caci Essence, Caci Gloves, Caci (Armor), Caci Kings Blade, Caci Kings Handle, Caci Kings Essence, Mischievous Mask

Draconic Blood
======================================================================
Page ID: 1638
Wiki revision: 2026-09-29T09:08:15Z

Categories:
- Perks

[Wiki Text]
Category:Perks 

__NOTOC__

[Raw Wikitext]
[[Category:Perks]] 
{{PerkTemplate
|Tags = Rune, Offensive, Buff, Debuff, RNG, Cooldown Reduction, Attack Speed, Healing, Heal Boost, Cleanse, Guardbreak, Poise, Defensive, Damage Boost, Damage Type, Guild
|Description = Your Rune Infusion is locked into a Draconic Ability from the Draconic Shrine. Additional of this perk increases the Damage of your Draconic abilities. If you are not Dragon-Blooded, your Abilities are significantly weaker.
|Incorrect= If you are not Dragon-Blooded, your Abilities are significantly weaker.
|Details = 
* Gain a draconic rune from the [[Lost_Shrine#tabber-Draconic_Shrine|Draconic Shrine]] that scales with your draconic element, and has additional effect(s) based on your draconic element
===Dragon Claw===
*Base Damage is equal to <code>25 + 2.5 * perkamount</code>
**1.0 Damage Type
**1.0 Scaling
**Base '''Poise''' Damage is equal to <code>60</code>
*Base Healing (<b>Holy</b>) is equal to <code>5 + 0.5 * perkamount</code>
*Base Healing (<b>Water</b>) is equal to <code>1.923 + 0.1923 * perkamount</code>
*5 Second Cooldown
*Guardbreaks
===Dragon Infusion===
<ul><li> Grants the {{StatusEffects|Draconic Infusion}} effect</li>
<li> All attacks gain +0.1 damage type of your respective draco color per 0.1 buff potency</li>
<ul><li> if you are not [[Races#tabber-Dragon_Blooded|Dragon Blooded]], instead gain physical damage type.</li>
<li> Does not apply to attacks without proc coefficient</li></ul>
<li>Gains bonus effects based on [[Races#tabber-Dragon_Blooded|Dragon Blooded]] Color</li>
<ul><li><b>Air</b>: Increases Attack Speed by 10% and Knockback by 20% per 0.1 {{StatusEffects|Draconic Infusion}} potency</li>
<li><b>Fire</b>: Attacks gain a 100% chance to apply burn, and increase applied burn potency by 15% per 1 of this perk</li>
<ul><li>Chance to apply burn is affected by proc coefficient.</li></ul>
<li><b>Hex</b>: Increases applied debuff potency by 5% and duration by 15% per 1 of this perk</li>
<li><b>Holy</b>: Increases healing by 10% per 0.1 {{StatusEffects|Draconic Infusion}} potency, and increases applied buff potency by 5% per 1 of this perk</li>
<li><b>Water</b>: Become immune to debuffs, and release a pulse every <code>8 - 1 * perkAmount</code> seconds that heals and cleanses</li>
<ul><li>Pulse base <b>Heal</b> is equal to <code>0.1</code></li>
<ul><li>1.0 <b>Water</b> scaling</li></ul></ul>
<li><b>Earth</b>: Increases <b>Poise</b> damage dealt by 15% and Stun Resistance by 0.15 per 0.1 {{StatusEffects|Draconic Infusion}} potency</li></ul>
<li> Lasts for 20 seconds</li>
<li> 35 Second Cooldown</li></ul>
===Dragon Bubble===
*Base Damage is equal to <code>20 + 2 * perkamount</code>
**1.0 Damage Type
**1.0 Scaling
**Base '''Poise''' Damage is equal to <code>45</code>
*Base Healing (<b>Holy</b>) is equal to <code>4 + 0.4 * perkamount</code>
*Base Healing (<b>Water</b>) is equal to <code>1.538 + 0.1538 * perkamount</code>
*7 Second Cooldown
*Goes through walls
*Detonates immediately upon contact with an enemy
|Notes = 
* Damage Type and Scalings of abilities depends on your skin color as a [[Races#tabber-Dragon_Blooded|Dragon Blooded]]
** (1) Red is <b>Fire</b>, (2) White is <b>Air</b>, (3) Green is <b>Earth</b>, (4) Blue is <b>Water</b>, (5) Yellow is <b>Holy</b>, (6) Purple is <b>Hex</b>
**If you are not Dragon Blooded the type is '''Physical'''
* Affected by [[Perk Effectiveness]]
===Attack Stats===
<ul><li><b>The following properties only apply to Dragon Claw and Dragon Bubble, not Infusion</b></li>
<li>Red (<b>Fire</b>): x1.2 Speed, Applies Burn</li>
<li>White (<b>Air</b>): x2 Speed, x2 Knockback, x0.75 Cooldown</li>
<ul><li>Directly lowers Cooldown by 25% without diminishing returns</li></ul>
<li>Green (<b>Earth</b>): x1.5 Damage, x1.5 Stun, x0.7 Speed</li>
<li>Blue (<b>Water</b>): x1.1 Damage, x1.2 Speed, x1.2 Stun, Heals and Cleanses</li>
<li>Yellow (<b>Holy</b>): Heals</li>
<li>Purple (<b>Hex</b>): 1.2x Stun, applies {{StatusEffects|Weakness}} alongside 3 chances to apply a random debuff</li>
<ul><li>Weakness potency is equal to <code>0.1 * draconicbloodAmount</code></li>
<li>Random debuffs can be any of the following :</li>
<li>{{StatusEffects|Bleed}}</li>
<li>{{StatusEffects|Burn}}</li>
<li>{{StatusEffects|Poison}}</li>
<li>{{StatusEffects|Shatter}} at 0.2 potency</li>
<li>{{StatusEffects|Slowness}} at 0.2 potency</li>
<li>{{StatusEffects|Weakness}} at 0.5 potency</li></ul></ul>
|Preview = Dragon Claw, Dragon Infusion, Dragon Bubble
|Sources = Draconic
}}

<ul><li>Uses the <code>damageTaken/(1+dmgReduction%)</code> formula</li>

|Notes =

|Sources = Grandmagic Great Blade, Grandmagic Long Handle, Grandmagic Great Essence

* Below is the average number of additional Weapon Art casts you get from this perk:
**+0.17 at 1 of this perk
**+0.33 at 2 of this perk
**+0.53 at 3 of this perk
**+0.81 at 4 of this perk
**+1.22 at 5 of this perk
*This is obtained by using the <code>chance/(1-chance)</code> formula and is the average number of additional weapon art casts you get from whatever amount of this perk. Realistically speaking, this does not mean your weapon art build will do 2.22x damage with 5 of this perk due to a variety of factors but it’s useful data for those who care about the numbers.

Penance

25%, 50%, 35%, 5 seconds

[Wiki Text]
Category:Perks 
PerkTemplate
Tags = Damage Boost, Debuff, Armor
Description = Gain a Damage Boost scaling on how little HP you have left, which maxes out at 25%. Additionally, whilst at 25% or lower, all hits have a 50% chance to inflict Bleed.
Incorrect= 25%
Details = 
Increases damage dealt by up to 25% depending on how low your HP is per 1 of this perk
Despite what the perk description says, the damage bonus maxes out at 35% of remaining HP
The damage boost per perk amount is equal to f(x)=\begin{cases}0.25&\text{if }\frac{0.25(1-x)}{0.65}\geq0.25\\\frac{0.25(1-x)}{0.65}&\text{if }\frac{0.25(1-x)}{0.65} where x = HP%
Gain a 50% chance to apply on hit when at or below 35% HP
Bleed duration is equal to 5 seconds
Formula=PenanceFormula
Notes =
- Despite what the in-game description states this perks effects max out when the player has 35% of their hp remaining, and not 25%
Preview = Penance
Sources = Atonement

Possession
======================================================================
Page ID: 1874
Wiki revision: 2026-05-14T12:09:53Z

Categories:
- Perks
- Incomplete

[Wiki Text]
Category:Perks Category:Incomplete

__NOTOC__

[Raw Wikitext]
[[Category:Perks]] [[Category:Incomplete]]
{{PerkTemplate
|Tags = Weapon Art Activated, Rune Activated, Debuff, Summoner, Incomplete, Weapon
|Description = Hitting a mob with a Weapon Art or Rune marks them for Possession. If the mob is killed with the mark, and you possess enough of a combination of this perk and Summon Boost, they can revive as your minion.
|Formula=PossessionFormula
|Details = 
<ul><li>Applies {{StatusEffects|Possession}} to enemies struck by weapon arts or runes</li>
<ul><li>Counts as a debuff</li></ul>
<li>Possessed mobs have a base HP equal to their natural counterparts</li>
<li>Possessed mobs have base damage equal to their natural counterparts</li></ul>
===Formula for Possession Power Requirement===
<code>([3 * Player Level] + Summon Boost + 50) * Perk Amount + 25</code><br>
<div style="width: 100%;">
[[File:Bawx-Possession1.png|frameless|upright=1.3|link=]]<br>
[[File:Bawx-Possession2.png|frameless|upright=1.3|link=]]
</div>
*To possess a mob, the number you get from this formula has to be greater than or equal to the mob’s '''base HP'''
*Enemy rarity and dungeon modifiers do not change the base HP of that mob.
===Formula for Possessed Mob HP===
<code>effectiveHP = baseHP * (1 + summonBoost% * (1+playerLevel/80))</code>
*Where baseHP = natural max HP of the possessed mob
**(i.e. a possessed Buni has a base HP of 25 which is equal to the max HP of a normal Buni)
===Formula for Possessed Mob Damage===
<code>damage = baseDMG * (1 + summonBoost% * (1+playerLevel/80))</code>
*“damage” is just the base dmg with summon scaling, does not account for any other factors
*This is the same formula for every summon’s summon scaling unless specified otherwise
|Notes = 
* See the [[Summons]] page for more info
|Preview = Possession
|Sources = Possessed Blade, Necrotic Handle, Possessed Essence
}}

|Details = 
===Formula===
(Remove any “%”s when calculating)</br>
<b>Dexterity Boost Gained =</b> <code>PerkAmount * 2/21 * (w + d * 3 + t * 0.4 + o * 2)</code>
* w = negative warding total
* d = negative physical and magic defense total
* t = negative tenacity
* o = negative other stats total
|Notes = 
* Speed Boost gained is equal to 10% of the dexterity boost gained

Poison Potency

Category:Perks 

__NOTOC__

[Raw Wikitext]
[[Category:Perks]] 
{{PerkTemplate
|Tags = Poison Potency, Debuff, Armor, Weapon, Ring
|Description = Increases the damage of the poisoned status by x%
|Details = 
<ul><li>Multiplies damage dealt by {{StatusEffects|Poison}} by :</li>
<ul><li>When perkAmount is less than 10 : <code>((1 + perkAmount/10) ^ (1 + perkAmount/10))</code></li>
<li>When perkAmount is greater or equal to 10 : <code>((1 + perkAmount/10) ^ (2))</code></li></ul>
<li>Increases applied {{StatusEffects|Poison}} potency by 0.1 per 1 of this perk</li></ul></ul>
|Notes = 
<ul><li>Not directly increased by [[Cursed (Perk)|Cursed]] or [[Perk Effectiveness]], however they do increase the amount of the source which indirectly increases the amount of this perk</li>
<li>The value given by the in-game perk description is <span style="color:red; font-weight:bold;">incorrect</span> and does not reflect the actual damage boost granted by this perk, and uses the following formula :</li>
<ul><li>When perkAmount is less than 10 : <code>((perkAmount/10) ^ (1 + perkAmount/10)) * (1 + perkAmount/15)</code></li>
<li>When perkAmount is greater or equal to 10 : <code>((perkAmount/10) ^ (2)) * (1 + perkAmount/15)</code></li></ul></ul>
|Preview = 
|Sources = Snake Venom Pole, Venomous Blade, Venomous Essence, Rotwood Pole, Poisonous Blade, Rot Essence, Spectral Handle, Spectral Blade, Spectral Essence, Murkwater Handle, Murky Blade, Murkwater Essence, Mungal Handle, Mungal Mallet, Mungal Essence, Mungal Pole, Mungus Core, Proto Essence, Spider Handle, Spider Fang Blade, Spider Essence, Snailsman's Noggin, Proto-Mungus (Armor), Puffball (Armor), Rot Wood Mage, Sporeling (Armor), Deep Spider (Armor), Deep Spider Gloves, Mischievous Mask, Rot Wood Fighter, Arachnid, Infectious Ring
}}

<li>Damage dealt by this effect does not take into account the Damage Type or post-Damage Type Multipliers of the attack that activated this perk, but can benefit from damage type bonuses and post-Damage Type Multipliers itself</li>

<li>Cannot proc other effects</li></ul>
<li>Bonus Air and Fire damage do not apply to attacks without Proc Coefficient</li>

Perfection
======================================================================
Page ID: 3677
Wiki revision: 2026-09-22T21:47:31Z

- Perks

[Wiki Text]
Category:Perks

[Raw Wikitext]
[[Category:Perks]] 
{{PerkTemplate
|Tags = Buff, Damage Boost, Crit, Crit Damage, Defensive, Enchantment, Mobility
|Description = Dodging or parrying, grants a stack of the Buff ''Perfection'', it increases your speed, critical chance and damage. Taking damage that wasnt parried or dodged reduces a stack.
|Details =
<ul><li>Dodging or [[Parry|Parrying]] an attack grants {{StatusEffects|Perfection}}</li>
<ul><li>Potency gained per dodge or parry is equal to <code>perkAmount</code> up to a maximum of <code>perkAmount*5</code> potency</li>
<li>{{StatusEffects|Perfection}} lasts indefinitely until taking an unblocked or undodged attack, lose <code>perkAmount</code> potency when taking an unblocked/undodged attack</li></ul>
<li>{{StatusEffects|Perfection}} grants the following bonuses per 1 potency</li>
<ul><li>Increases damage dealt by 2%</li>
<li>Increases crit chance and crit damage by 1%</li>
<li>Increases movement speed by 3%</li></ul></ul>
|Notes =
*'''Perfection''' stacks are '''not reduced''' by self-inflicted damage.
**A bug currently exists with [[Cantrips#tabber-Expunge|Expunge]] and [[Crimson Tithe]] where they both can consume stacks of Perfection at random.
* Does not work with [[Divine Intervention]]
* Crit Chance from this perk works with [[Primal]]
* Despite what the in-game status description displays, Perfection grants 2% damage bonus per 1 potency, not 1.5%
* '''Max stack at 1 perk'''
** Increases damage dealt by 10%
** Increases crit chance and crit damage by 5%
** Increases movement speed by 15%
* '''Max stack at 5 perk'''
** Increases damage dealt by 50%
** Increases crit chance and crit damage by 25%
** Increases movement speed by 75%
|Preview = 
|Sources = Flawless Enchantment
}}

[Raw Wikitext]
[[Category:Perks]] 
{{PerkTemplate
|Tags = Damage Boost, Weapon
|Description = Each LMB grants the following hit of the combo a Damage Boost. Resets after the LMB combo ends or repeats.
|Details = 
* Increases damage dealt by M1s/M2s by 15% per M1/M2 per perk 
** This is per M1/M2, and not per hit, e.g. at one of the perk the first M1/M2 in a combo deals 15% more damage, the second deals 30% more damage, and the third deals 45% more damage
** This bonus maxes out on the third M1/M2 in a chain, and resets when the M1/M2 combo repeats or restarts
|Notes = 
* Compatible with [[Delta Drill]], but does not increase the damage boost past the maximum
* Compatible with [[Deltabit]]
* Damage Bonus only applies to the weapon attacks themselves
* Damage Bonus applies regardless of whether an enemy was hit, and applies from the first M1/M2 in the combo
** i.e. starts from a 15% bonus on the first M1/M2, rather than a 0%
* Works with M2s, despite the omission in the perk description
* Savage Combo does not grant a damage bonus to the following effects, despite being tied to an M1/M2 input
** [[Divine Crash]]
** [[Fell Rush]]
** [[Ichor Spark]]
** [[Mine]]
** [[Spirit Commune|Monk Spirits]]
** [[Steam Charge]]
|Preview = 
|Sources = Crude Blade, Sturdy Handle, Crude Essence
}}

PerkTemplate
Tags = Spirit (Monk), Guild
Description = LMB's, RMB's, mob kills and Poisebreaks grants Spiritual Energy. Spiritual Energy can be released in various forms, depending on the glove.
Incorrect=Poisebreaks
Details = 
Gain when attacking
The amount of stacks gained depends on spirit commune perk amount, the attack, the type of spirit, and your attack speed, more details below
Spiritual Energy is used to activate monk spirits, with different activation conditions depending on the specific spirit
Spirit Commune Formula
spiritCoeff * attackCoeff * (1 + (perkAmount - 1) * 1/6) / (AttackSpeedMultiplier)
- spiritCoeff is the spirit gain multiplier of a spirit/glove, for the full list see below
- attackCoeff is the spirit gain multiplier of an attack, for the full list see below
- perkAmount is Spirit Commune perk amount
- AttackSpeedMultiplier is equal to your attack speed after all modifiers
- See Attack Speed for more information

======================================================================
Static Buildup
======================================================================
Page ID: 1977
Wiki revision: 2026-09-30T20:21:45Z

Categories:
- Perks

[Wiki Text]
Category:Perks 

__NOTOC__

[Raw Wikitext]
[[Category:Perks]] 
{{PerkTemplate
|Tags = Offensive, Finisher,Guardbreak, Weapon
|Description = Hitting enemies visibly builds up a yellow charge on your Weapon. Landing an RMB calls down multiple lightning strikes, the amount of which scales off of your charge.
|Details = 
* Lightning Strike Damage is equal to <code>(3 + (rmbDamage * 0.1538)) * (1 + 0.1 * perkAmount)</code>
** 0.5 '''Air''' Damage Type, 0.5 '''Magic''' Damage Type
** 1.0 '''Air''' Scaling, 1.0 '''Magic''' Scaling
** rmbDamage is inherited after the Effective Boost Multiplier and Level Damage Bonus are applied, but before other damage bonuses
*** i.e. Lightning Strikes do not inherit damage increases that affected the proccing RMB, but can be affected by damage bonuses themself
*** See [[Damage Calculation]] for more information

* Any hit that has a Proc Coefficient will grant 1 charge, up to a maximum of 20 charges
** Charges are only consumed when you have at least 3 charge
** Charges are generated and then consumed, i.e. LMB, LMB, RMB, is three charges, and spawns a strike
* Lightning Strike amount is equal to :
** if <code>Charge < 3</code>, then <code>0</code> strikes
** if <code>20 > Charge ≥ 3</code>, then <code>(1 + (Charge/20) * perkAmount))</code> strikes
** if <code>Charge = 20</code>, then <code>(1 + (Charge/20) * perkAmount * 2))</code> strikes
**Strike Amount is always rounded down
|Notes = 
* Cannot proc other effects
* Guardbreaks
|Preview = Static Buildup
|Sources = Lightning Hammer, Thunderwood Handle, Lightning Essence
}}

{{PerkTemplate
|Tags = Damage Boost, Attack Speed, Damage Type, Enchantment
|Description = Grants a hefty amount of Earth Damage Type on your Weapon, but reduces Attack Speed accordingly. This reduction can be mitigated by Earth Boost.
|Incorrect= This reduction can be mitigated by Earth Boost.
|Details = 
* Gain +0.3 '''Earth''' Damage Type on the user's weapon per 1 of this perk
** Adds directly to weapon damage type, attacks that use the weapon's damage type will gain this bonus
* Reduces Attack Speed by 7% per 1 of this perk
** Reduction follows the <code>AttackTime * (1 + 0.07 * perkAmount)</code> formula
** This reduction affects weapon arts, even ones that don't gain the bonus damage type
** Multiplicative with all other attack speed modifiers, see [[Attack Speed]]
** In spite of the perks description, '''Earth''' boost does nothing to this penalty
|Notes = 
* Only applies Damage Type to attacks that use the weapon's damage type
** i.e. Weapon Attacks, and certain [[Weapon Arts]]
** Does not apply to attacks that ''match'' weapon damage type, i.e. having 1.0 earth damage type on your weapon does not mean it will apply to perks like [[Quake]] or [[Pulverizing Rush]]
* Changes your weapon's metal texture to stone
* Additionally works on attacks that change to the user's highest damage type, like Pure Magic [[Weapon Arts]], [[Magic Missile Rune]] and [[Locked And Loaded]]
** However save for [[Locked And Loaded]], most of these attacks only have damage type equivalent to 1.0 of the users highest damage type, and will not gain additional damage type from this perk
|Preview = Stone Weapon
|Sources = Stone Enchantment
}}

{{PerkTemplate

|Description = Your LMB's and RMB's are replaced with chained lightning blasts.
|Details =

* M2 Base Damage is equal to <code>2.75 * 5</code>
** Has a reduced chance to proc other effects (0.6 proc coefficient)
** Procs finisher perks such as [[Tempest]] half as many times
* Both M1s and M2s proc chain lightning, see below for more details
===Chain Lightning===
<ul><li>Chain Lightning deals damage equal to <code>1/6</code> of the original attacks damage to the initial target, and enemies it chains to</li>
<ul><li>0.5 <b>Air</b> Damage Type, 0.5 <b>Magic</b> Damage Type</li>
<li>Damage dealt by this effect is unaffected by Level Bonus Multiplier and Damage Bonus multiplier, but can indirectly benefit from those effects by inheriting increased damage from the original hit</li>
<li>Damage dealt by this effect does not take into account the Damage Type or post-Damage Type Multipliers of the attack that activated this perk, but can benefit from damage type bonuses and post-Damage Type Multipliers itself</li>
<ul><li>See [[Damage Calculation]] for more information</li></ul>
<li>Chains prioritize the furthest target within its range, and can only chain to each target once</li>
<li>Chains are capable of chaining to the original target</li>
<li>Only one instance of chain lightning is triggered per attack, regardless of how many targets were hit</li>
<li>Can chain to up to 2 additional targets</li>
<li>Cannot proc other effects</li></ul></ul>
|Notes = 
* LMB combo has no finisher
|Preview = Storm Caster M1, Storm Caster M2
|Sources = Lightning Stratos Core, Lightning Stratos Essence
}}

|Incorrect=66%
|Details = 
*Increases Holy and Hex boost multiplicatively by 50%
**This happens after holy and hex boost is averaged
**Perk description is not up to date
*Increase damage dealt by 0.125% per 1% '''Holy''' Boost per 1 of this perk
**Damage Increase = <code>1/8 * perkAmount * holyBoost</code>
*Increase healing by ~0.0666% per 1% '''Hex''' Boost per 1 of this perk

===Debuffs -> Buffs===

<li>The list of corresponding buffs are as follows:</li>
<ul><li>{{StatusEffects|Bleed}} / {{StatusEffects|Burn}} / {{StatusEffects|Poison}} -> {{StatusEffects|Regen}}</li>
<li>{{StatusEffects|Frostbite}} / {{StatusEffects|Slowness}} / {{StatusEffects|Sticky}} -> {{StatusEffects|Tailwind}}</li>
<li>{{StatusEffects|Shatter}} / {{StatusEffects|Electrical Rend}} / {{StatusEffects|Hypnotized}} -> {{StatusEffects|Reinforce}}</li>
<li>{{StatusEffects|Weakness}} -> {{StatusEffects|Rage}}</li>
<li>{{StatusEffects|Despair}} -> {{StatusEffects|Inspired}}</li></ul>
<li>{{StatusEffects|Regen}} has a potency equal to <code>0.1 * perkAmount</code> and has a duration of <code>1 + perkAmount</code> seconds</li>
<li>{{StatusEffects|Tailwind}} has a potency equal to <code>0.1 * perkAmount</code> and has a duration of <code>3 + perkAmount</code> seconds</li>
<li>{{StatusEffects|Reinforce}} has a potency equal to <code>0.1 + 0.1 * perkAmount</code> and has a duration of <code>5 + perkAmount</code> seconds</li>
<li>{{StatusEffects|Rage}} has a potency equal to <code>0.1 * perkAmount</code> and has a duration of <code>3 + perkAmount</code> seconds</li>
<li>{{StatusEffects|Inspired}} has a potency equal to <code>0.1 *perkAmount</code> and has a duration of <code>4 + perkAmount</code> seconds</li></code></li></ul></ul>
|Notes = 
<ul><li>Does not give Buffs when applying self-debuffs besides for {{StatusEffects|Despair}}</li>
<ul><li>This is because most self-debuffs aren't 'inflicted by the player', in the same way other statuses are</li></ul>
|Preview = 
|Sources = Infused Knight

{{PerkTemplate
|Tags = Offensive, Debuff, Finisher, Damage Boost, Weapon
|Description = Your Finishers shoots out a venomous blast that inflicts Poison. Additionally, gain a Damage Boost against Poisoned opponents.
|Details = 
<ul><li>Increases damage dealt to enemies inflicted with {{StatusEffects|Poison}} by 10% per 1 of this perk</li>
<li>Base Damage is equal to <code>2 + 0.6 * perkAmount</code></li>
<ul><li>1.0 '''Hex''' Damage Type</li>
<li>1.0 '''Hex''' Scaling, 1.0 '''Earth''' Scaling</li>
<li>Applies {{StatusEffects|Poison}}</li></ul>
<li>Base damage is reduced depending on the finisher hit it's fired on</li>
<ul><li>Multiplies Base Damage by <code>1 / (1 + (finisherNumber-1) / 3)</code></li>
<li> e.g. at 1 of the perk the first blast of venom (first finisher hit) will have <code>2.6 / (1 + (1-1) / 3)</code> or <code>2.6</code> Base Damage, and the second blast of venom (second finisher hit) will have <code>2.6 / (1 + (2-1) / 3)</code> or <code>1.95</code> Base Damage and so on</li></ul></ul>
|Notes = 
* Cannot proc other effects
* Activates on all finisher hits, but half as many times with [[Locked And Loaded|Dual Guns]] or [[Storm Caster]] M2
** These finishers still count the finisher hit number for the formula even if it doesn't create a blast of venom
|Preview = Venom Spitter
|Sources = Venomous Blade, Snake Venom Pole, Venomous Essence
}}

|Description = Converts all Tailwind to the Buff ''Whirlwind'', with an increased duration. ''Whirlwind''. Additionally, using a Weapon Art or Rune release a wind slash around yourself that inflicts Bleed.
|Details = 
<ul><li>Converts {{StatusEffects|Tailwind}} to {{StatusEffects|Whirlwind}}</li>
<li>{{StatusEffects|Whirlwind}} increases movement speed and jump boost by 6.66% per 0.1 potency</li>
<li>Whirlwind provides 30% Weapon Art and Rune cooldown reduction per 1 of this perk, and is an effect <b>entirely unrelated to the buffs potency</b></li>
<ul><li>Uses the <code>cooldown/(1+cooldownReduction%)</code> formula</li>
<li>See [[Cooldowns]] for more details</li></ul>
<li>AoE Wind Blade base damage is equal to <code>3 + 0.3 * perkAmount</code></li>
<ul><li>1.0 '''Air''' Damage Type</li>
<li>1.0 '''Air''' Scaling</li>
<li>Applies {{StatusEffects|Bleed}} for 5 seconds</li>
<li>Can proc other effects</li>
<li>Does not require the user to have {{StatusEffects|Whirlwind}} to activate</li></ul></ul>
|Notes = 
<ul><li>Unlike [[Void Rage]], [[Frenzy]] or similar perks, which just add a bonus effect to their buff, {{StatusEffects|Whirlwind}} is a completely separate buff from {{StatusEffects|Tailwind}}</li>
<li>{{StatusEffects|Whirlwind}} still works with perks that require {{StatusEffects|Tailwind}} to function, like [[Spirit Winds]], [[Engine]], etc.</li></ul>
|Formula=Cooldowns
|Preview = Whirlwind
|Sources = Gale Blade, Gustiron Handle, Gale Essence
}}

** 1.0 '''Air''' Damage Type
** 1.0 '''Air''' Scaling
|Notes = 
* Speed scales with your own movement speed
** [[Thief Training]] also increases the speed of this perk, however its effect of halving roll cooldown appears to not work while using this perk
* Flies in the direction of the user's camera
** Cannot be aimed too high, and aiming too high or low greatly lowers speed
* Can be held for up to 2 seconds, dealing damage every 0.2 seconds
* Does not affect [[Combat Roll Rune]]
* Can proc other effects
* Keeps i-frames for the same duration as base roll
